Resampling-based analyses characterize this distribution, revealing which steps of a rollout determine how the model arrives at its answer. However, a major limitation of these approaches is that resampling text sequences at every token or sentence in a reasoning chain is very costly. Our work strives to make resampling analysis more computationally efficient, while also shedding light on an important scientific question: what is the right statistical model for explaining uncertainty dynamics in text generation? We show that when resampling many reasoning chains, uncertainty dynamics converge to stable patterns, and noise is largely an artifact of sampling rather than an LLM's sensitivity to each individual token or reasoning step. We develop a statistical model for smoothing noisy low-sample rollout data to better approximate high-sample data, allowing us to significantly cut sampling costs.

</details>


### [73] [Mitigating Identity Essentialism in LLM Agents with Longitudinal Life Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19621)
*Hexi Wang,Yujia Zhou,Bangde Du,Weihang Su,Xinyuan Cao,Qingyi Pan,Qingyao Ai,Yueyue Wu,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出静态画像（static-profile）智能体在LLM社会模拟中表现出比人类更强的群体分离与组内压缩（身份本质主义），并提出LifeMem——一种结合结构化生活事件检索与智能体专属参数化记忆的纵向记忆框架，在三个LLM、两个数据集上改善响应分布、整体/组内多样性及跨生命阶段个体变化与人类数据的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM为大规模社会模拟提供了可扩展途径，但其可信度取决于智能体的构造方式。现有方法能部分复现群体层面的模式，却往往无法捕捉人类特有的多样性；静态画像智能体因身份标签引发的本质主义倾向会抹平组内个体差异，需设计更贴近人类认知的记忆机制。

Method: 受互补记忆系统启发，LifeMem将长期记忆框架解耦为两条通路：结构化生活事件检索（提取关键生命事件形成可检索的情节性记忆）与智能体专属参数化记忆（持续整合个体经验）。通过二者协同，使智能体不再依赖稀疏静态画像与仅靠提示的短期记忆，从而实现动态演化。

Result: 在Add Health与Understanding Society两个数据集、三个LLM上的实验表明：LifeMem在响应分布、整体与组内多样性、以及跨生命阶段个体层面的响应变化模式等方面均比现有方法更贴近人类数据，同时缓解了身份本质主义导致的静态化与同质化问题。

Conclusion: 纵向生活事件记忆对构建更忠实、动态演化的社会智能体具有重要价值；将"身份即本质"的静态表征替换为随经历整合的经验性记忆，是提升LLM社会模拟可信度的有效方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable approach to social simulation, but their credibility depends on how agents are constructed. Existing methods can partially reproduce population-level patterns, yet often fail to capture human-like diversity. Our analysis shows that static-profile agents exhibit stronger demographic separation and within-group compression than humans, a pattern consistent with identity essentialism: demographic labels can encourage models to treat group-average tendencies as individual traits, homogenizing responses within groups. We argue that this limitation arises from two related factors: sparse, static agent representations and the limited ability of prompt-only memory to persistently integrate experience. Inspired by complementary memory systems, we propose LifeMem, a longitudinal memory framework that combines structured life-event retrieval with agent-specific parametric memory for experience integration. Experiments on Add Health and Understanding Society with three LLMs show that LifeMem improves alignment with human data in terms of response distributions, overall and within-group diversity, and patterns of within-person response change across life stages. These findings highlight the value of longitudinal life-event memory for constructing more faithful and dynamically evolving social agents.

</details>


### [74] [ReCache: Efficient KV Cache Reuse and Compression for Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19662)
*Yichu Fang,Sitong Wei,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReCache 是一个针对智能体（agentic）语言模型的缓存框架。它通过资源级注意力（resource-wise attention）将可复用的工具/技能 schema 编码与具体资源访问分离，生成组合不变的 KV 块，从而实现独立缓存并减少推理时的计算与内存开销。在七个公开工具/技能使用数据集上，其资源级注意力与密集调用性能相当（Inv-F1 82.3% 对 82.4%），并带来 3.655× 的首 token 延迟加速；完整框架将分配的 KV 张量内存降低 92.43%，并将注意力加速 1.423×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体语言模型会在不同请求中以不同的组合和顺序反复编码工具与技能 schema，这些可复用部分使得标准前缀缓存无法重用其 KV 状态，导致推理开销显著增加。现有方法缺少对“可复用 schema 编码”与“特定资源访问”的分离，本文旨在独立缓存资源表示并降低其推理时的计算与内存负担。

Method: 提出 ReCache 框架，包含三项关键技术：1) 资源级注意力（resource-wise attention）：去除跨资源交互并为每个资源分配局部位置，从而产生“组合不变”的 KV 块，使资源表示可独立缓存；2) 通过贡献选择的层—KV 头组路径（contribution-selected layer–KV-head-group routes）限制资源可见性，只让必要的注意力路径访问资源；3) 通过结构性与语义性剪枝仅保留调用关键字段（invocation-critical fields），进一步压缩缓存内容。整体实现“缓存可重用 schema 编码 + 选择性资源访问”的思路。

Result: 在由七个公开工具/技能使用数据集（含资源不相交测试）构成的基准上，资源级注意力达到 82.3% 的 Inv-F1，与密集调用性能（82.4%）几乎持平；同时实现 3.655× 的首 token 时间延迟（time-to-first-token）加速。完整 ReCache 框架将分配的 KV 张量内存减少 92.43%，并将注意力计算加速 1.423×。

Conclusion: 实验表明，将可复用的 schema 编码与选择性资源访问分离，能够在不显著损失效果（约 0.1 个 Inv-F1 点）的前提下大幅降低智能体模型的推理成本，验证了 ReCache 作为高效智能体推理基础设施的有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Agentic language models repeatedly encode tool and skill schemas that recur across requests in different combinations and orders, preventing standard prefix caching from reusing their key--value (KV) states. We introduce \textbf{ReCache}, a framework for independently caching resource representations while reducing their inference-time computational and memory overhead. Resource-wise attention removes cross-resource interactions and assigns resource-local positions, producing composition-invariant KV blocks. ReCache then restricts resource visibility to contribution-selected layer--KV-head-group routes and retains only invocation-critical fields through structural and semantic pruning. We evaluate ReCache on a benchmark assembled from seven public tool- and skill-use datasets, including resource-disjoint tests. Resource-wise attention matches dense invocation performance (82.3\% versus 82.4\% Inv-F1) while providing a 3.655$\times$ time-to-first-token speedup. The complete framework reduces allocated KV-tensor memory by 92.43\% and accelerates attention by 1.423$\times$. These results show that separating reusable schema encoding from selective resource access substantially reduces agentic inference costs with limited effectiveness loss. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/ReCache.

</details>


### [75] [The Asymmetric Harms of LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19670)
*Yuan Wu,Mairui Li,Lesia Semenova,Chudi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了3个大语言模型在11种压缩方法下的行为变化，发现压缩会不对称地损害知识保留（头部知识比尾部知识损失更严重）、使模型对丢失的知识保持过度自信，并让稳定的平均偏见分数掩盖不同人口统计子群体间相反的刻板偏好转变。这些行为变化无法被困惑度和准确率等聚合指标捕获，凸显了部署前进行细粒度评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM压缩虽然降低了部署成本，但标准的聚合指标（如困惑度、准确率）往往掩盖了压缩带来的底层行为偏移。研究旨在揭示压缩在知识保留、模型置信度和社会偏见等方面导致的、传统指标无法反映的细微且不对称的变化。

Method: 对3个LLM在11种压缩方法下进行系统评估，分别从知识保留（区分头部知识与尾部知识）、模型置信度（针对新丢失知识的回答置信度）和社会偏见（不同人口统计子群体的刻板偏好）三个维度展开分析，并将结果与标准聚合指标进行对照。

Result: 压缩对头部知识的相对保留率的削减程度大于尾部知识；压缩后的模型对其新丢失知识给出的错误答案往往仍然保持高度自信；总体偏见得分看似稳定，但实际上掩盖了不同人口统计子群体之间显著且方向相反的刻板偏好偏移。

Conclusion: 压缩引发的行为变化是不对称的，且无法通过聚合性能指标捕捉。因此，在部署压缩模型之前需要进行细粒度的、面向具体维度的评估，而不能仅依赖困惑度、准确率等总体指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) compression reduces deployment costs, but standard aggregate metrics like perplexity and accuracy often mask underlying behavioral shifts. In this work, we systematically evaluate 3 LLMs across 11 compression methods to investigate the effects of compression on knowledge retention, model confidence, and social bias. We find that compression disproportionately reduces the relative retention of head knowledge compared to tail knowledge. Furthermore, compressed models often remain substantially confident in their incorrect answers on newly lost knowledge. Finally, we demonstrate that stable aggregate bias scores can conceal substantial, opposing shifts in stereotypical preferences across demographic subgroups. Together, these findings reveal asymmetric behavioral changes that aggregate performance measures fail to capture, highlighting the need for granular evaluation of compressed models before deployment.

</details>


### [76] [Projector Is All You Train](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19726)
*Nyx Iskandar,Saathvik Selvan,Slater Victoroff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）适配新模态时是否必须微调语言模型主干。通过 3D MLLM 实验发现，仅训练投影层（projector）就足以取得与联合训练相当甚至更强的多模态性能，同时避免语言模型既有能力的退化，且训练吞吐量约为联合训练的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 MLLM 训练流程同时适配语言模型主干和投影层，但这一做法是否是必要且最优的尚不明确；联合微调主干可能带来不必要的计算开销和语言能力漂移，因此作者希望探究仅训练投影层是否能充分完成新模态适配。

Method: 在 3D MLLM 设定下进行对照实验：将仅训练投影层的方案与使用相同编码器和主干的联合训练基线（两者均训练主干和投影层）及现有基线模型进行公平比较；并在多种语言模型主干上，通过 3D 分类/描述基准以及语言、视觉、空间推理等标准基准进行验证。

Result: 仅训练投影层即可达到相对现有基线和联合训练模型而言的强多模态性能；联合训练会导致语言模型既有能力的漂移退化，而仅训练投影层天然避免该问题；同时投影层训练的训练样本吞吐量约为联合训练的两倍。

Conclusion: 对于 MLLM 适配新模态，微调语言模型主干并非必要；仅训练投影层是一种更高效、更稳健的适配策略，兼具性能、能力保持和训练效率方面的优势。

Abstract: The typical training process of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) involves adapting both the language model backbone and the projector between the backbone and a modality-specific encoder. We ask whether fine-tuning the backbone of an MLLM is necessary to adapt it to a new modality. Through experiments on 3D MLLMs, we find that training only the projector is sufficient to achieve strong multimodal performance relative to existing baseline models and our jointly trained MLLMs with the same encoder and backbone. We also show that joint training leads to undesirable drift in existing capabilities of the language model, which projector-only training avoids by definition. Furthermore, projector-only training has approximately twice the training sample throughput of joint training. We validate our findings across different language model backbones via 3D classification and captioning benchmarks as well as standard benchmarks evaluating language, vision, and spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [77] [One Success Isn't Reliability: Thinkingbox, a Sandbox and Benchmark for Agents in Stateful Business Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19741)
*Zhuochun Li,Youngmin Ko,Ali Keramati,Nicola Ferri,Susana Palmaz Lopez Pelaez,Liang-Chun Tsai,Calvin Wang,Mirco Milletari,Tuhin Kundu,Vadim Smolyakov,Kjartan Olafsson,Tommy Guy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Thinkingbox——一个隔离的、兼容 MCP 的"工具-智能体-用户"交互沙盒，提供完整执行轨迹和基于终端后端状态的结局评估；并基于它构建 Thinkingbox-bench，包含 507 个受策略约束的工作流（覆盖零售、酒店、车险、新银行内部 IT、咨询 IT/HR 等场景），通过任务特定的可执行检查评估。最强模型 pass@1 为 65.36%，但 pass@20 仅 25.25%，大量失败轨迹以"干净终止 + 有效状态变更"告终，说明响应或工具调用层面的信号并不能代理端到端任务完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多在可执行环境中评测，但完成代码之外的实质性业务工作不仅需要生成合理的响应或合法工具调用，还需要多轮收集缺失信息、遵循领域策略、协调相互依赖的工具，并在不造成副作用的前提下实现正确的持久状态迁移。作者希望构建一个能评测完整业务任务结局（而非仅中间动作）的沙盒和基准。

Method: 构建 Thinkingbox 沙盒：提供隔离的 MCP 兼容工具会话、完整执行轨迹，以及针对终端后端状态进行结局评估。在此之上构建 Thinkingbox-bench：507 个策略条件化工作流跨多个领域；每个尝试由任务特定的可执行检查评测，接受合法轨迹但拒绝错误、缺失或多余的效果；指定任务还额外检查最终响应的必需属性。评测涵盖专有和开放权重模型。

Result: 最强模型 pass@1 达到 65.36%，但 pass@20 仅为 25.25%；许多失败的尝试表现出干净的终止和有效的状态变更动作，表明以响应或工具调用级信号无法清晰代理端到端任务完成。基准揭示"偶然找到成功轨迹"与"可靠完成有状态业务任务"之间存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: Thinkingbox-bench 表明现有模型在真实业务任务中端到端完成能力仍不可靠，需要更严格的结局级评测来刻画智能体能力；作者开源 Thinkingbox 与 Thinkingbox-Bench 以推动该方向的研究。

Abstract: Recent agent benchmarks increasingly ground evaluation in executable environments, from code repair to web navigation, app APIs, and function calling. Yet completing consequential work beyond code requires more than producing a plausible response or valid tool call: agents must gather missing information over multiple turns, follow domain policies, coordinate dependent tools, and realize the correct persistent state transition without collateral effects. In this paper, we introduce Thinkingbox, a sandbox for tool-agent-user interaction that provides isolated MCP-compatible tool sessions, complete execution traces, and outcome evaluation over terminal backend state. Built on this sandbox, Thinkingbox-bench contains 507 policy-conditioned workflows across numerous scenarios, including retail, hospitality, auto insurance, neobank internal IT, and consulting IT/HR support. Each attempt is evaluated by task-specific executable checks that accept valid trajectories while rejecting wrong, missing, or extra effects; designated tasks additionally check required properties of the final response. Across proprietary and open-weight models, the strongest achieves 65.36% pass@1, but only 25.25% pass^20. Moreover, many failed trials show clean termination and valid state-changing actions, showing that response or tool-call-level signals are not clear proxies for end-to-end task completion. Thinkingbox-bench reveals a large gap between occasionally finding a successful trajectory and reliably completing stateful business tasks. We release both Thinkingbox and Thinkingbox-Bench: https://github.com/microsoft/thinkingbox

</details>


### [78] [PersonalBench: Measuring the Authorship Gap in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19746)
*Yash Ganpat Sawant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PersonalBench，一个专门评测推理时个性化文本生成方法是否真正接近目标作者写作风格的基准。现有基准只衡量任务准确率或偏好对齐，而非输出是否酷似目标作者。PersonalBench通过三重视角（LUAR作者身份验证模型、LLM-as-judge、自动化文体测量）在50位作者、1000次生成、两个模型家族（Qwen 3、GLM-4）上评测。主要发现：个性化方法确实产生作者可区分的输出（LUAR AUC=0.918），但所有方法都无法跨越人类-LLM边界——所有方法对真实作者的LUAR相似度为0.484-0.508，低于跨作者人类下限0.626（上限0.756）。LLM自身的作者指纹占主导，生成文本与任何人类作者的差距大于随机人类之间的差距。各方法在LUAR上统计上不可区分（差距0.024），但在LLM裁判上看似有区分度，这种矛盾可追溯到特质提取与档案提取之间的循环性。作者验证了LUAR在该语料库上可靠（单帖AUC=0.76，多帖0.96）。结论：推理时个性化只是调制llama风格，无法弥合与人类作者的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化文本生成研究使用的基准要么衡量任务准确性，要么衡量偏好对齐（如LLM是否符合用户偏好），却没有真正检验模型输出是否在写作风格上与目标作者相似。作者认为这才是"个性化"的核心目标，因此需要一个经过校准、可复现的评测标准来回答"模型输出究竟有多接近目标作者"这一本质问题。

Method: 构建PersonalBench基准，包含50位作者、1000次生成，覆盖Qwen 3与GLM-4两个模型家族。评测采用三条独立途径：(1) LUAR——经过训练的作者身份验证模型；(2) LLM-as-judge——用LLM做风格评判；(3) 自动化文体测量（stylometrics）。同时验证LUAR自身的可靠性（单帖AUC=0.76，多帖AUC=0.96），并追踪LLM裁判与LUAR结果不一致的根源（特质提取与档案提取的循环性）。

Result: 个性化方法能产生作者可区分的输出（LUAR区分目标作者AUC=0.918），但没有任何方法跨越人类-LLM边界：所有方法对真实作者的LUAR相似度均在0.484-0.508，低于跨作者人类下限0.626（上限0.756）。LLM自身作者指纹主导输出，生成文本比随机人类彼此间的距离更远离任何人类作者。各方法在LUAR上统计上无法区分（仅0.024的差距），尽管LLM裁判显示它们有差异——该矛盾源于特质提取与档案提取的循环性。

Conclusion: 推理时个性化方法只能调制（modulate）LLM固有的写作风格，在统计上彼此等价，且都无法弥合与人类作者之间的鸿沟。PersonalBench被发布作为经过校准的"标尺"（measuring stick），供后续研究以统一标准衡量个性化是否真正实现人类作者身份复制。

Abstract: Personalized text generation aims to make LLMs write in a specific individual's style, yet existing benchmarks measure task accuracy or preference alignment rather than whether the model's output actually resembles the target author's writing. We introduce PersonalBench, a benchmark that evaluates inference-time personalization methods through three independent lenses: LUAR (a trained authorship verification model), an LLM-as-judge, and automated stylometrics. Across 50 authors, 1,000 generations, and two model families (Qwen 3, GLM-4), we find that personalization methods do produce author-differentiated output (LUAR discriminates target authors within generated text at AUC=0.918) but this differentiation never crosses the human-LLM boundary. All methods achieve LUAR similarity to real authors in the range 0.484-0.508, below the cross-author human floor of 0.626 (ceiling 0.756). The LLM's own authorship fingerprint dominates: generated text is more distant from any human author than random humans are from each other. Methods are statistically indistinguishable from each other on LUAR (spread 0.024) despite appearing differentiated on the LLM judge, a discrepancy we trace to circularity between trait extraction and profile extraction. We validate that LUAR reliably measures authorship in our corpus (AUC=0.76 single-post, 0.96 multi-post). We release PersonalBench as a calibrated measuring stick: inference-time personalization modulates the LLM's style but does not bridge the gap to human authorship.

</details>


### [79] [FlashPrefill V2: Block-Sparse Prefill Attention for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19758)
*Qihang Fan,Huaibo Huang,Zhiying Wu,Bingning Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlashPrefill V2，将先前 FlashPrefill 算法原型从三个维度推向生产级长上下文推理：引入均值校正项抑制稀疏化近似误差；基于 PackGQA 访存、warp 特化和 pingpong 流水线重新设计稀疏注意力算子，全面对标 FlashAttention-3/4 并支持 FP8 推理；原生支持分页 KV Cache 与连续批处理，可集成进 SGLang 等现代推理框架。在 NVIDIA H20 上，128K 上下文长度下较 FlashAttention-2 最高提速 47.26x（FP8）与 27.19x（BF16），FP8 下较 FA3/4 对齐的稠密基线仍达 30.49x 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文建模是大语言模型的关键能力，但注意力机制的二次复杂度在计算密集的预填充阶段构成严重瓶颈。先前工作 FlashPrefill 虽能通过即时模式发现与基于最大值的动态阈值化降低开销，但仍是算法原型，距离生产部署尚有显著差距。

Method: FlashPrefill V2 从三个维度进行演进：(1) 引入均值校正项（mean correction term）有效抑制近似误差，使极端稀疏度下性能退化可控；(2) 采用 PackGQA 访存布局、warp 特化与 pingpong 流水线重设计稀疏注意力算子，全面对齐最新的 FlashAttention-3/4 实现，并支持 FP8 推断以满足实际量化需求；(3) 原生支持分页 KV Cache 与连续批处理，使其可作为注意力后端无缝集成到 SGLang 等现代推理框架中。

Result: 在广泛部署的 NVIDIA H20 推理加速器上的大量评测表明：128K 上下文长度下，FlashPrefill V2 在 FP8 与 BF16 精度下分别较 FlashAttention-2 取得最高 47.26x 与 27.19x 加速；在 FP8 下对照 FA3/4 对齐的稠密基线，仍可获得 30.49x 加速。

Conclusion: FlashPrefill V2 成功将稀疏注意力算法从原型推向生产级实践，在精度、算子效率与框架集成性三个层面均取得实质进展，为长上下文预填充阶段的高效部署提供了可直接落地的注意力后端方案。

Abstract: Long-context modeling is a pivotal capability for Large Language Models, yet the quadratic complexity of attention remains a critical bottleneck, particularly during the compute-intensive prefilling phase. Our previous work, FlashPrefill, mitigates this cost through instantaneous pattern discovery and max-based dynamic thresholding; however, it remains an algorithmic prototype that is still distant from production deployment. In this paper, we present FlashPrefill V2, which evolves FlashPrefill from a prototype toward practical long-context serving along three dimensions. First, we introduce a mean correction term that effectively suppresses the approximation error, keeping performance degradation manageable even at extreme sparsity levels. Second, we redesign the sparse attention operator with PackGQA memory access, warp specialization, and pingpong pipelining, fully aligning with the latest FlashAttention-3/4 implementations and supporting FP8 inference to meet practical quantization requirements. Third, FlashPrefill V2 natively supports paged KV cache and continuous batching, allowing integration as an attention backend in modern inference frameworks such as SGLang. Extensive evaluations on NVIDIA H20 GPUs---among the most widely deployed inference accelerators---demonstrate that FlashPrefill V2 delivers up to 47.26x and 27.19x speedups over FlashAttention-2 at 128K context length under FP8 and BF16 precision, respectively, and, in FP8, still achieves a 30.49x speedup against an FA3/4-aligned dense baseline.

</details>


### [80] [SWE-bench Science: Can Coding Agents Resolve Engineering Tasks in Science?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19799)
*Zhipeng Xu,Jiahao Lu,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SWE-bench Science，一个面向科学软件工程领域的仓库级基准，包含来自 20 个科学领域、98 个 GitHub 仓库的 119 个任务，并按三个范式（Issue-driven、Expert-exploratory、Engineering-integration）组织。即使最优的编码智能体（带 Opus-5 max 的 Claude Code）pass@1 仍低于 50%，说明科学软件修复极具挑战。论文识别出四类重复出现的失败机制，并通过配对消融实验发现：科学知识的收益并不一致——良好的信息可约束修复并提升平均性能与 token 效率，而错位的引导会诱发锚定效应。该基准为研究编码智能体在科学软件工程中的能力与失败机制提供了广泛测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件日益成为科学仪器本身的组成部分，科学代码的故障不仅危害程序行为，还可能危及支撑科学结论的证据。然而现有编码智能体评估大多只关注聚合任务成功率，难以解释智能体在修复科学软件时为何失败，缺乏针对科学软件工程这一高风险领域的系统性诊断与基准。

Method: 构建 SWE-bench Science 基准：从 20 个科学领域、98 个仓库收集 119 个任务，划分为 Issue-driven、Expert-exploratory、Engineering-integration 三个范式；对最先进智能体（Claude Code + Opus-5 max）进行评估；分析失败案例归纳四类失败机制；并设计配对消融实验——移除显式科学引导但保留仓库与可执行工程上下文，对比有无科学知识指导下的修复表现。

Result: 最优智能体 pass@1 低于 50%，表明科学软件工程仍具显著挑战。识别出四类失败机制：科学知识或抽象能力不足、探索误入歧途或仅做表面修复、修复覆盖不完整或系统集成缺失、无法将科学知识泛化到未观测案例。消融实验显示科学知识并非一律有益：良好对齐的信息可约束修复空间、提升平均性能与 token 效率；而错位引导会诱发锚定并未必提升精确修复成功率。

Conclusion: SWE-bench Science 为科学软件工程中的编码智能体能力与失败机制研究提供了广阔测试平台；研究还提示科学知识在智能体修复中的双刃剑效应——知识引导的价值取决于其质量与对齐程度，未来应关注何时以及如何注入科学知识以提升修复可靠性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Software increasingly functions as part of the scientific instrument itself, making failures in scientific code capable of compromising not only program behavior but also the evidence underlying scientific conclusions. Yet existing evaluations of coding agents largely emphasize aggregate task success, providing limited insight into why agents fail when repairing scientific software. We introduce \textbf{SWE-bench Science}, a repository-level benchmark for scientific software engineering comprising 119 tasks from 98 GitHub repositories across 20 scientific domains. Each task is organized into one of three paradigms: Issue-driven, Expert-exploratory, and Engineering-integration. Even the best-performing agent, \textbf{Claude Code with Opus-5 (max), achieves a pass@1 below 50\%}, highlighting the substantial challenges posed by scientific software engineering. We identify four recurring failure mechanisms: deficits in scientific knowledge or abstraction, misguided exploration or surface-level repair, incomplete repair coverage or system integration, and failures to generalize scientific knowledge beyond observed cases in our analysis. We further conduct a paired ablation that removes explicit scientific guidance while preserving the repository and executable engineering context. The results show that scientific knowledge is not uniformly beneficial: well-grounded information can constrain repair and improve average performance and token efficiency, whereas poorly aligned guidance can induce anchoring and does not necessarily improve exact repair success. Together, SWE-bench Science provides a broad testbed for studying both the capabilities and failure mechanisms of coding agents in scientific software engineering.

</details>


### [81] [LoRA-GA$^2$: Low Rank Adaptation with Multi-step Gradient Adaptive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19800)
*Haonan He,Xinyue Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoRA-GA²是一种高效微调算法，通过轻量级探针获取预训练权重的多步梯度信息，并结合频谱感知的秩分配与最优初始化，在保持vanilla LoRA效率优势的同时，持续超越现有LoRA变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽以低内存开销取得与全量微调相当的性能，但仍存在持续的性能差距。现有方法（如利用一步梯度近似对齐LoRA更新与全量微调主方向）未能捕获梯度的完整动态，导致性能提升受限。

Method: 提出多步梯度利用框架：1）设计轻量级探针用于获取预训练权重的多步梯度，该探针不引入额外GPU显存成本且时间开销极小；2）基于多步梯度进行频谱感知（spectrum-aware）的重要性秩分配；3）利用多步梯度推导最优初始化方案。

Result: 实验结果表明LoRA-GA²在多个基准上持续超越现有LoRA变体：GLUE基准上平均领先最强基线0.66分，GSM8K上领先1.03分，HumanEval上领先0.87分，同时保留vanilla LoRA的高效性。

Conclusion: 通过充分利用多步梯度动态而非单步近似，LoRA-GA²有效弥合了LoRA与全量微调之间的性能差距，证明了多步梯度信息结合频谱感知秩分配与最优初始化是实现高效高精度微调的有效途径。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a prominent fine-tuning method for large models, achieving competitive performance with reduced memory overhead. However, a persistent performance gap remains between LoRA and full fine-tuning. Recent studies have sought to narrow this gap by employing one-step gradient approximations of pretrained weights to align LoRA updates with the principal directions or intrinsic dimensionalities of full fine-tuning updates. Nevertheless, these approaches fail to capture the full dynamics of the gradients. In this paper, we propose LoRA-GA$^2$, an effective fine-tuning algorithm that fully leverages multi-step gradient information. Specifically, we introduce a lightweight probe for multi-step gradients of pretrained weights that incurs no additional GPU memory cost and only marginal time overhead. We further employ a spectrum-aware, importance-based rank allocation and optimal initialization derived from multi-step gradients. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LoRA-GA$^2$ consistently outperforms existing LoRA variants while preserving the efficiency advantages of vanilla LoRA. For instance, LoRA-GA$^2$ surpasses the leading baseline by an average of 0.66 points on the GLUE benchmark, and outperforms the strongest baseline by 1.03 points on GSM8K and 0.87 points on HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


### [82] [Stopping and Routing LLM Judge Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19802)
*Bin Zhu,Yi Xie,Yanghui Rao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将LLM评估流水线中的评审（judge）面板设计建模为角色条件分配问题：基于小规模标注审计集、声明的数据切片和评审成本，估计各评审的相对角色（副本、互补者、专家），并据此生成可复用的评审调用策略——丢弃冗余副本、全局添加互补评审、按条件路由专家评审、在验证增益低于阈值时停止。跨推理、代码、安全、偏好、奖励模型、摘要和数学审计基准，与多种基线比较后得到“评审调用机制图”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估流水线常包含大量候选评审（通用LLM评判提示、奖励模型、安全分类器、置信度变体、任务专用验证器等）。部署问题不仅在于哪个评审最好，更在于应调用哪些评审、在哪些样本上调用、以及何时停止构建评审面板——这些决策缺乏统一的数学框架和可审计的方法。

Method: 将评审面板设计形式化为“角色条件分配问题”。从少量标注审计集、声明切片和评审成本出发，估计目标相对角色：副本（无新增条件信息）、互补者（改进全局面板）、专家（仅在某切片上发挥效用）。由此推导策略：丢弃副本、全局添加互补者、按条件路由专家、当验证增益低于阈值时停止构建。

Result: 在推理、代码、安全、偏好、奖励模型、摘要和数学审计任务上，与单评审、扁平面板、匹配多样性启发式、全调用堆叠、可靠性陪审团和节俭级联等基线对比。产出了评审调用机制图：在可部署切片上路由专家、在饱和验证器机制中停止、在风险收益值得时保留广泛集成、忽略条件副本。最终输出为下一批评估批次可复用、可审计的调用计划。

Conclusion: 该方法为多评审评估面板提供了一种数据驱动、成本感知且可审计的设计与调度方式，超越了简单的“哪个评审最好”问题，将选择、路由与停止决策统一到角色估计框架中，并给出针对不同评估场景的明确调用策略。

Abstract: LLM evaluation pipelines often have many candidate judges: general LLM-as-a-judge prompts, reward models, safety classifiers, confidence variants, and task-specific verifiers. The deployment question is not only which judge is best, but which judges should be called, on which examples, and when panel construction should stop. We formulate judge-panel design as a role-conditioned allocation problem. From a small labeled audit set, declared slices, and judge costs, the method estimates target-relative roles: copies add no conditional information, complements improve the global panel, and specialists help only on slices. These roles induce a policy: drop copies, add complements globally, route specialists conditionally, and stop when validation gain falls below a threshold. Across reasoning, code, safety, preference, reward-model, summarization, and math audits, the method is compared with single judges, flat panels, matched diversity heuristics, full-call stacking, reliability juries, and frugal cascades. The result is a regime map for judge calls: route specialists on deployable slices, stop in saturated verifier regimes, keep broad ensembles when their risk benefit is worth the cost, and ignore conditional copies. The output is a reusable, auditable call plan for the next evaluation batch.

</details>


### [83] [A knowledge-guided agentic framework for mitigating patient-context ambiguity in health queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19875)
*Mahyar Abbasian,Saba A. Farahani,Arshia Ilaty,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对医疗健康聊天机器人中患者查询语境不明确的问题，本文提出了一种知识引导的智能代理框架（knowledge-guided agentic framework）。该框架位于患者与下游语言模型之间，通过任务专用知识图谱构建合理假设、识别缺失的患者语境变量并提出针对性追问，最后将原始查询与获取到的语境合并为澄清后的提示词，再交由下游模型作答。在五个语言模型、两个受控基准（诊断检索与饮食安全分类）上的实验表明，该框架显著提升了回答准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者常提交简短、信息不充分的查询，这些查询虽然语言清晰，但可能因未披露的症状、诊断、用药、过敏或饮食限制等因素而支持多种看似合理的答案。若语言模型直接作答，可能基于未经证实的假设，导致误导性或不安全的回答。

Method: 提出一个位于患者与未改动下游语言模型之间的智能代理框架。其流程为：1) 解析初始查询；2) 利用任务专用知识图谱构建一组合理的假设；3) 识别区分这些假设所需的缺失患者语境变量；4) 提出针对性的追问以获取语境；5) 将原始查询与获取的语境合并为澄清提示词，输入下游模型生成最终响应。

Result: 在五个语言模型上、采用两个受控的消歧基准进行评估（1,034条系统性地掩蔽临床相关证据的症状查询用于诊断检索；487条省略关键健康语境的查询用于饮食安全分类）。与直接提示相比，诊断检索中整体精确Top-1准确率至少提高57.1个百分点，选择性精确Recall@5至少提高77.7个百分点；饮食安全分类中五个模型的准确率均有提升，且四个模型取得最高Matthews相关系数。

Conclusion: 通过在下游模型生成最终响应前主动获取缺失的患者语境信息，该框架能有效缓解患者-语境歧义，显著优于直接回答和仅换一种措辞而未获取新信息的方式，从而提升医疗问答的安全性与准确性。

Abstract: Patients often submit short, underspecified queries to healthcare chatbots that lack the patient-specific information needed to determine an appropriate response. Although these queries may be linguistically clear, they can support multiple plausible answers depending on undisclosed factors such as symptoms, diagnoses, medications, allergies, or dietary restrictions. A language model answering such a query directly may therefore rely on unsupported assumptions about the patient. We introduce a knowledge-guided agentic framework for mitigating patient-context ambiguity before final response generation. The framework operates between the patient and an otherwise unchanged downstream language model. It interprets the initial query, uses a task-specific knowledge graph to construct a set of plausible hypotheses, identifies the missing patient-context variables needed to distinguish among them, and asks targeted follow-up questions. The original query and the acquired context are then combined into a clarified prompt for the downstream model. We evaluated the framework across five language models using two controlled ambiguity-mitigation benchmarks: diagnosis retrieval from 1,034 symptom queries with clinically relevant evidence systematically masked, and dietary-safety classification from 487 queries with decisive health context omitted. The framework was compared with direct answering of the underspecified query and with rephrasing the same query without acquiring new patient information. In diagnosis retrieval, it increased overall exact Top-1 accuracy by at least 57.1 percentage points and selective exact Recall@5 by at least 77.7 percentage points across the five evaluated models compared with direct prompting. In dietary-safety classification, it improved accuracy across all five models and achieved the highest Matthews correlation coefficient for four...

</details>


### [84] [Interrupting the Loop: Periodic Subject Changes Raise Judged Surprise and Connection in Base Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19893)
*Roberto I. Ono Filho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在24种条件下、基于三个基础模型解构一个受认知启发的生成循环，发现其绝大部分效果来自单一操作：每隔数百个token注入一个新主题（中断），并配合抑制字面重复的机制（习惯化）。在仅评判生成文本窗口、以命题为单元、经复现性校验的评测协议下，中断使惊喜度提升1.2–1.4分、连接度提升0.8分；而要求连续性的连接词有害，纯段落分隔无效果，重置上下文不劣于保留上下文。窗口评判器无法察觉的三件事——注入句被误判为模型自身输出、固定轮换注入导致模型重放评判视界之外的旧段落（在150–300周期时65–80%的中断后窗口被误判为惊喜与连接）、以及局部增益无法组合成整合文档——均值得一般性关注。在带验证器的在线装箱问题中，中断使有效且不同的候选启发式数量提升3–4倍，但不提升最优解质量。论文提供长文本生成的评测协议及对简单干预的受控刻画，而非创造力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基础语言模型在无任务条件下的"新颖性"来源，并厘清一个长文本流上的LLM评判器究竟能"看见"什么。作者希望将一个受认知科学启发的生成循环系统解构，识别其效果的真实载体，并提出可靠的长文本生成评估协议，以规避窗口式评判器的盲区。

Method: 在三个基础模型上运行24种条件的生成循环，将效果逐一拆解到单个操作（每几百token注入新主题的中断、抑制重复的习惯化）。评估仅基于生成的文本窗口，以命题为单元（n=10），评判器经过可重复性度量，并对照第二个评判器族与人类读者。设有预注册的复制实验；另在有验证器的问题（在线装箱）上检验候选启发式的数量与质量。

Result: 中断在习惯化之上使评判的惊喜度提升1.2–1.4分、连接度提升0.8分；要求连续性的连接词降低效果，纯段落分隔无可见增益，重置上下文效果不劣于保持上下文，预注册复制确认了主要对比。发现三个窗口评判器盲区：注入句被判定为模型自身输出；固定轮换注入使模型重放评判视界外的前期片段，且被评判为惊喜与连接（150–300周期时占中断后窗口65–80%）；局部增益不合成整合文档。显著性监视器、循环内评判器、跨中断记忆及门控Review运行均无增益。在线装箱中中断将有效且不同的候选启发式数量提升3–4倍，但最佳质量不提升。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于给出一种面向长文本生成的评测协议，以及对一个简单干预（中断+习惯化）的受控刻画，而非提出创造力机制。结果提醒研究者：窗口式评判存在系统性盲区，局部文本增益与整体文档质量脱节，评测信度需要专门的重复性度量和多评判器交叉验证。

Abstract: Where does the novelty a base language model produces with no task come from, and what can an LLM judge of a long stream actually see? We dismantle a cognitively inspired generation loop over 24 conditions on three base models. Most of its effect lives in one operation: a new subject injected every few hundred tokens (an interruption) into a stream whose literal repetition is damped (habituation). We judge windows of generated text only, with the premise as the unit (n=10) and a judge measured for repeatability, against a second judge family and against human readers. Under that protocol the interruption raises judged surprise by 1.2 to 1.4 points and connection by 0.8 over habituation alone. A connective that asks for continuity hurts; a bare paragraph break adds nothing detectable on fresh text; a reset context does at least as well as a kept one; and a pre-registered replication on new premises confirms the primary contrast. Three things the window judge could not see changed the first version of this study, and we think they are of general use. The judge scores the experimenter's injected sentence as the model's own. A fixed rotation of injected sentences makes the model replay its earlier segments from beyond the judge's horizon, and the judge scores the replay as surprise and connection (65-80% of post-interruption windows at periods 150-300). And the local gains do not compose: no arm produces an integrated document. The salience monitor, the in-loop judge, memory across interruptions and a judge-gated Review run with a gate that opens add nothing. On a problem with a verifier (online bin packing), the interruption multiplies valid, distinct candidate heuristics three- to fourfold without raising the quality of the best. We report an evaluation protocol for long generation and a controlled characterization of a simple intervention, not a mechanism of creativity.

</details>


### [85] [Learning how to Forget: Fine-tuning for Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19920)
*Matthias Seeger,Zeyu Zhang,Vihang Patil,Konstantinos Benidis,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的稀疏注意力微调方法，适用于任意KV缓存策略，仅需适度硬件预算（如单张40GB A100），使模型与缓存策略协同适应，常优于使用精确注意力（序列并行）训练的模型；并开源了KeysAndValues库，包含针对H2O稀疏注意力的高效SDPA内核实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多通过稀疏注意力进行KV缓存选择与压缩，以实现低成本的长上下文推理。然而，模型通常在生成阶段之前就已用精确注意力训练完成，推理时采用的稀疏缓存策略与模型训练方式不匹配，导致性能损失。本文动机是让模型在训练阶段就与KV缓存/稀疏注意力策略共同适应，从而在有限硬件预算下提升长上下文推理质量。

Method: 提出一种针对稀疏注意力微调模型的新方法：该方法与具体的KV缓存策略无关（适用于任意策略），在训练中引入稀疏注意力机制，使模型与缓存策略协同适应（co-adapt）。同时对实验中的领先策略H2O稀疏注意力提供了高效实现，配有专门的缩放点积注意力（SDPA）内核支持；并发布开源库KeysAndValues，涵盖长上下文推理与微调的易用且高性能代码。

Result: 实验表明，该稀疏注意力微调方法在中等硬件预算（例如单张NVIDIA A100 40GB）下即可运行，且在多数情况下优于使用精确注意力（序列并行）训练得到的模型。H2O作为实验中表现领先的策略，其专用内核实现提升了效率。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力微调是一种高效且实用的替代方案：它允许模型与任何KV缓存策略协同适应，以较低硬件成本超越精确注意力训练的模型，并通过开源库KeysAndValues（https://github.com/awslabs/keys_values）向社区提供了可复现的易用实现。

Abstract: A lot of prior work addressed key-value (KV) cache selection and compression by sparse attention to enable long-context inference for transformer language models without excessive hardware budgets. We provide a new method for fine-tuning models with sparse attention. It works for any KV cache policy, runs on a moderate hardware budget (e.g., a single Nvidia A100 GPU with 40 GB RAM), and allows the model to co-adapt with the policy, often outperforming models trained with exact attention (sequence parallelism). We also provide an efficient implementation of H2O sparse attention (the leading policy in our experiments) with dedicated scaled dot product attention kernel support. KeysAndValues (https://github.com/awslabs/keys_values), a new open source library for long-context inference and fine-tuning, provides easy-to-use and performant code for all methods discussed here.

</details>


### [86] [Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19942)
*Hao Guo,Subin Huang,Junjie Chen,Zhifa Geng,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的多模态讽刺检测（MSD）框架，结合动态门控跨模态融合与讽刺感知对比正则化（SaCR），以解决模态贡献实例依赖和表面语义一致性掩盖矛盾意图的问题，在MMSD和MMSD2.0数据集上持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测中，字面含义与上下文线索间的不一致常暗示反讽，但现有方法存在两大局限：（1）依赖固定的融合策略，无法捕捉实例级别的模态贡献差异；（2）将讽刺简单视为通用的跨模态不匹配，忽略了表面语义一致可能掩盖潜在矛盾意图的“误导性语义一致性”，难以捕获微妙的讽刺线索和实例特定的模态交互。

Method: 提出了一个集成动态门控跨模态融合与SaCR的MSD框架。具体包括：（1）双向门控交互模块，执行跨模态特征过滤并在实例级别自适应校准文本和视觉的贡献；（2）动态融合门，平衡模态重要性以生成更鲁棒的多模态表示；（3）SaCR作为标签感知的对比正则化目标，对非讽刺样本鼓励语义一致性，对讽刺样本抑制误导性一致性；（4）采用多目标学习策略端到端训练，联合优化多模态分类与辅助单模态监督。

Result: 在MMSD和MMSD2.0两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法在性能上始终优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过实例级自适应模态交互和标签感知对比正则化，该框架能有效区分表面语义一致与真实一致，从而提升多模态讽刺检测的准确性；双向门控融合与多目标学习策略共同支撑了其鲁棒性与有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection aims to identify sarcastic intent from multimodal content, where inconsistencies between literal meaning and contextual cues often signal irony. This task has attracted increasing research attention. However, accurate detection remains challenging due to instance-dependent modality contributions and misleading semantic consistency, where surface-level alignment masks underlying contradictory intent. Existing methods often rely on fixed fusion strategies and treat sarcasm as generic cross-modal mismatch, limiting their ability to capture subtle sarcasm cues and instance-specific modality interactions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MSD framework that integrates Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization (SaCR). Specifically, a bidirectional gated interaction module performs cross-modal feature filtering and adaptively calibrates textual and visual contributions at the instance level. A dynamic fusion gate further balances modality importance to generate more robust multimodal representations. Furthermore, SaCR is introduced as a label-aware contrastive regularization objective that encourages semantic consistency for non-sarcastic samples while suppressing misleading consistency in sarcastic cases. The proposed framework is trained end-to-end with a multi-objective learning strategy that jointly optimizes multimodal classification and auxiliary unimodal supervision. Extensive experiments on MMSD and MMSD2.0 demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [87] [Natural Language Code Retrieval for 1C:Enterprise: An Open Benchmark and Efficient Bi-Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19957)
*Konstantin Chesnokov,Chingiz Mingazov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向 1C:Enterprise 生态的完整代码检索流程，包括包含 3,413 个真实且经 PII 脱敏的查询-代码对的开源基准、可复现的评测框架，以及专用双编码器模型。为了解决标注数据稀缺问题，作者利用公开代码库和 gemma 模型生成 784,057 个合成三元组进行微调，并结合 Matryoshka 表示学习与隐私感知分词器。模型在 nDCG@10 上达到 0.5992（balanced macro）、0.5044（micro）和 0.4617（论坛子集），显著优于基线架构（0.4932）与 google/embeddinggemma-300m（0.5404）；重叠审计表明训练-基准重叠不构成主线结果的主要解释。将向量截断至 256 维可保留 99.9% 的检索质量，同时将稠密索引存储与精确相似度计算量降低三分之二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言代码检索虽在快速演进，但 1C:Enterprise 生态同时包含俄语语法和高度领域特化的术语，此前几乎不存在开源数据集和专门模型，导致该领域的研究与评估长期空白。

Method: 作者构建了端到端流程：1）发布 3,413 个真实、经 PII 脱敏的查询-代码对基准和可复现评测工具；2）利用 google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it 从公共代码仓库生成 784,057 个合成三元组以弥补标注不足；3）采用 Matryoshka 表示学习（MRL）与隐私感知分词器微调专用双编码器；4）使用保守的 exact/13-gram 重叠审计检验训练与基准数据重叠的影响，并报告 balanced macro、query-weighted micro 及论坛子集等多样化的评测指标。

Result: 专用模型在 nDCG@10 上取得 0.5992 balanced macro、0.5044 micro 和 0.4617 论坛子集结果，均优于基线架构（0.4932）和 google/embeddinggemma-300m（0.5404）。剔除被重叠审计标记的基准样本后，成绩为 0.6011 macro / 0.5010 micro，说明数据重叠不解释主线结果。MRL 截断至 256 维保留 99.9% 检索质量，并使稠密索引存储和精确相似度算术量减少至三分之一。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了 1C:Enterprise 代码检索在开放基准与专用模型方面的空白，验证了合成数据+领域微调+MRL 的组合在低资源领域中的有效性，并证明结果稳健性不受训练-基准重叠影响，同时 MRL 可在几乎无损的情况下大幅降低部署成本。

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>


### [88] [Robust Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis via Iterative Proxy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19971)
*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向不完整多模态情感分析（MSA）的迭代代理校正框架。该方法从非语言模态构造语言导向的代理表示，通过门控残差校正机制在多模态上下文中逐步精炼该代理，并依据估计的语言可靠性分数自适应融合代理与观测到的语言表征。同时引入分阶段潜在校正目标，以完整语言表征作为训练期语义锚点来稳定代理精炼轨迹。在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS数据集上的多种模态缺失设置下，该方法持续优于竞争基线，实现稳健的情感预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态输入常不完整或受损，削弱跨模态互补性，并向下游融合引入误导信息。现有基于代理的方法通常使用一次性代理构造来补偿退化的语言信息，但生成的代理初始化时可能粗糙或不可靠；过早注入该代理会传播初始误差，损害情感预测性能。因此需要一种能够渐进校正代理、平衡代理补偿与可信语言证据的框架。

Method: 1) 构造语言导向代理：从非语言模态构建针对语言的代表示。2) 迭代门控残差校正：在多模态上下文下通过门控残差机制逐步精炼代理，避免一次性注入导致的误差传播。3) 自适应融合：根据估计的语言可靠性分数，在代理补偿与观测到的真实语言表示之间进行平衡。4) 分阶段潜在校正目标：利用完整语言表示作为训练时的语义锚点，稳定代理精炼轨迹。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS数据集上，针对多种缺失模态设置的大量实验表明，该框架一致优于竞争基线，在不完整输入下实现了稳健的情感预测。

Conclusion: 迭代代理校正框架能够有效应对不完整多模态输入下的信息缺失问题，通过渐进式精炼和可靠性感知融合消除初始化误差的传播，显著提升MSA在现实场景中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>


### [89] [HealMed: Multilingual Evaluation of Large Language Models in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19981)
*Yingjian Chen,Fan Gao,Sherry T. Tong,Haoyu Zhang,Aosong Feng,Kevin W. Jin,Xing Wu,Jinghui Lu,Abdul Samad,Akbar Faruqi,Cesar Caraballo,Cibele Brandão,Dhruva,Gupta,Eunji Jeon,Gabriel Madera-Santiago,Geon Lee,Hugo Toshio Itikawa,Insook Cho,Isabelli Martins,Isarar Siddique,Israr Ahmed,Jihyo Kwak,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Luis Guilherme Cardoso,Minjin Kim,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Renee Dua,Santiago Gudiño-Rosales,Xiujie Chen,Zeo Lapalus,Zixin Xu,Michihiro Yasunaga,Rex Ying,Heuiseok Lim,Jaewoo Kang,Chanjun Park,Hang Jiang,Ethan Goh,Hyunjae Kim,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qingyu Chen,Irene Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HealMed 是一个由医学专家审核的多语言医学大语言模型评测基准，包含 9 种语言、每个语言 1000 个示例，覆盖 MCQA、NLI 和开放式问答三种任务格式。研究发现低资源语言上模型性能下降最明显，最强闭源模型跨语言最稳定，而许多开源和医学专用模型跨语言差距更大且不一致；医学专业化本身并不能保证多语言鲁棒性，专家修订可能提高或降低评测分数，表明翻译质量对跨语言评测结果有实质性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型评测大多集中于英语或少数高资源语言，缺乏经过专业审核、覆盖多语言的高质量基准，难以真实衡量模型在不同语言场景下的医学能力与跨语言鲁棒性。

Method: 由来自 9 个国家和地区的 23 名医生和医学专家历时两年构建基准，从九个数据集中抽取数据，覆盖 MCQA、NLI 和开放式问答三种任务格式，每种语言包含 1,000 个示例；每条翻译由精通英语和目标语言的两位专家进行评估和修订。

Result: 低资源语言上模型性能下降最大，但下降幅度因语言和模型而异；最强闭源模型跨语言最稳定，而许多开源和医学专用模型表现出更大且不一致的差距。医学专用化并不能保证多语言鲁棒性。专家修订对评测分数的影响方向不定（可升可降），说明翻译质量显著影响跨语言评测结果。

Conclusion: HealMed 提供了一个严谨、专家审核的多语言医学评测基准，并揭示了当前模型在多语言医学场景下的关键短板：低资源语言性能下降、医学专用模型缺乏跨语言稳定性，以及翻译质量对评测结论的重要影响。这些发现对医学 LLM 的评估方法论和多语言部署实践具有指导意义。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>


### [90] [Auditing Cross-Lingual Fairness in Language Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20047)
*Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个面向多语言LLM水印方案的多维度评估框架，涵盖经验校准的检测阈值、阈值无关的伴随测量、三种互斥的质量评估范式以及基于类型学语系划分的广义熵分解。在6种水印方案、3个开源生成器、11种语言（覆盖4种文字、8个语系）及两种训练模式下验证，发现跨语言公平性差距主要源于语言的结构性属性而非特定语言的特异性，且单语言单范式评估无法揭示这些失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM输出水印方案几乎只在英语文本上评测，且仅依赖各方案自身的检测阈值和狭窄的质量测量集。这种评估设计缺陷在英语上不显现，但在多语言部署中会左右结论。因此需要一个能跨语言可靠评估水印方案的方法框架，以识别单语言、单范式评测无法暴露的失效模式与公平性差距。

Method: 提出四组件评估框架：(1) 按部署情境经验校准检测阈值；(2) 引入阈值无关的伴随测量，以区分校准失败与检测失败；(3) 采用三种互斥的质量测量范式——分布型、配对语义型、参考困惑度型；(4) 基于类型学语系划分做广义熵分解，量化跨语言差异的来源。实验覆盖6种水印方案、3个开源权重生成器、11种语言（4种文字、8个类型学语系）、基座与指令微调两种模式。

Result: 该框架揭示了单语言单范式评估无法发现的失效模式。在检测与质量两个维度上，观测到的跨语言差异在类型学语系划分上以语系间差异为主，说明水印的跨语言公平性差距源于语言的结构性属性，而非特定语言的特异性现象。

Conclusion: 跨语言水印评估必须采用多组件框架，且水印方案的跨语言公平性差距在结构上与语言的类型学属性相关。面向多语言部署的水印设计应关注语系层面的结构性公平，而非个别语言的特异性改进。

Abstract: Watermarking schemes for large language model output are evaluated almost exclusively on English text using each scheme's detection threshold and a narrow set of quality measurements. Multilingual deployment exposes evaluation-design choices that are inconsequential on English but determine conclusions cross-lingually. We propose an evaluation framework with four components: detection thresholds calibrated empirically per deployment context, a threshold-independent companion measurement that distinguishes calibration failures from detection failures, three disjoint quality measurement paradigms (distributional, paired-semantic, and reference-perplexity), and a generalized-entropy decomposition of cross-language disparity over a typological family partition. Applied to six watermarking schemes, three open-weight generators, eleven languages spanning four scripts and eight typological families, and both base and instruction-tuned regimes, the framework reveals failure modes that single-language single-paradigm evaluation cannot surface. Across detection and quality, observed disparity is predominantly between-family on the typological partition, indicating that cross-lingual fairness gaps in watermarking are structural to language properties rather than idiosyncratic to particular languages.

</details>


### [91] [SABET-QA: Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20083)
*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SABET-QA，一种面向时序知识图谱问答（TKGQA）的迭代多跳推理框架。它通过双向实体-时间打分机制与槽位感知上下文模块，将问题语义与时序知识图谱嵌入对齐，并利用可微分工作记忆渐进优化推理假设，辅助时序边界作为粗粒度监督。在 CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ 和 TimeQuestions 四个基准上超越强基线，尤其在复杂多步时序查询上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱问答需要对时间敏感的事实进行多步推理，而现有基于嵌入的方法采用单遍（single-pass）推理管线，难以处理多跳查询，缺乏跨步骤的状态精化能力，导致在复杂时序问题上的推理能力受限。

Method: SABET-QA 包含三个核心组件：(1) 双向实体-时间打分机制，对跨多个跳的推理状态进行迭代精化；(2) 槽位感知上下文化模块（slot-aware contextualization），将问题语义与时序 KG 嵌入对齐；(3) 可微分工作记忆，支持渐进式假设精化。此外，在可用时利用辅助时序边界作为粗粒度监督信号。

Result: 在 CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ 和 TimeQuestions 四个数据集上的实验表明，SABET-QA 在多个强基线上取得了一致的性能提升，尤其是在复杂多步时序查询场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 迭代式多跳推理配合双向实体-时间评分与工作记忆机制，能有效弥补现有单遍嵌入方法在时序 KG 问答中的不足，是处理复杂时序多步查询的有力方案。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [92] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OenoBench是首个综合性葡萄酒领域知识基准，包含来自政府注册机构（INAO、TTB、OIV）、同行评审期刊及Wikipedia/Wikidata的3,266道多选题，覆盖六大知识支柱和四个难度层级。其核心方法学贡献是"LLM驱动但绝不以LLM为事实来源"的构建管线：每条事实都溯源到URL，由35个来源可验证的抓取器从38,104条原子事实提取，问题由五种生成策略和五个生成器家族生成，并经九智能体审计（以Cohen's κ对齐人工金标准）。在16个前沿配置上的评测发现：总体准确率53%-84%（o3最高83.6%），推理模式增益集中于DeepSeek R1，且存在显著的自偏好/逆偏好偏差，揭示了封闭式可解题项的参数化回忆天花板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用知识基准在专业垂直领域（如葡萄酒）难以评估模型的事实准确性，且多数基准构建依赖LLM生成内容，缺乏严格的可溯源性与真值锚定，容易引入幻觉或数据污染。作者旨在构建一个完全来源锚定、可审计、覆盖领域专家知识维度的葡萄酒基准，以解决"LLM是否真正掌握受监管领域事实"这一评测缺口，并量化不同推理模式与模型家族的系统性偏差。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 语料构建：35个可验证来源抓取器从INAO、TTB、OIV、同行评审期刊和Wikipedia/Wikidata提取38,104条原子事实，全部锚定URL；(2) 题目生成：采用"LLM管线"——语言模型仅负责将已验证事实重塑为问题并审计结果，绝不作事实来源；题目由五个生成器家族、五种生成策略产生324万候选并经九智能体审计，审计结果用Cohen's κ与人工金标准校准；(3) 评测：在六大支柱（产区、葡萄品种、葡萄栽培、酿酒、生产商、商业）和四个难度层级上评估16个前沿模型配置，并引入封闭式可解/情境式切片来分析参数化回忆与上下文检索能力。

Result: 主要发现：(i) 总体准确率跨度53%-84%，o3以83.6%领先；(ii) 推理模式提升集中在DeepSeek R1（+6.8个百分点），在Claude Opus和Gemini Pro上无提升或缺失；(iii) 显著的来源偏好偏差：Anthropic在其自有题目上自偏好+9pp，Google则呈-8pp逆偏好；(iv) 前沿开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率Pareto前沿，即性价比相当；(v) 所有配置在封闭式可解题项上仅增益约33pp，揭示参数化回忆的天花板效应，只有情境化切片可突破。

Conclusion: 该工作证明在受监管专业领域内可构建完全可溯源、审计严格的知识基准，LLM可作为格式转换与质检工具而不充当真值来源。基准揭示了前沿模型在领域事实回忆上的系统性天花板与家族间偏好偏差，说明此类"封闭式知识"评测与情境增强切片是互补的评估维度。作者以CC-BY-SA-4.0发布语料、审计发现、人工审查应用及构建代码，为垂直领域基准提供开放复现范本。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>

### [93] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督机器学习的新型直升机起飞重量估计模型，利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集，并按照EASA机器学习应用概念文件和Eurocae ED-324标准实施了学习保证流程；通过提出机器学习需求、模型描述及LSTM循环神经网络的实现并进行需求验证，最终在传统航电计算机上演示了该模型在机载目标（如前视告警等关键功能）部署的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直升机的关键飞行功能（如机载告警）中，起飞重量是重要参数，而传统估计方法可能依赖地面装载数据，缺乏实时、准确的机载估计能力。同时，航空领域机器学习应用需要满足适航审定要求，尚无成熟统一的标准与保证流程，因此需要一种既能在机载环境运行、又符合监管要求的重量估计方法。

Method: 采用监督式机器学习框架，结合空客全球在役直升机机队的大规模运行数据集构建模型；遵循EASA机器学习概念论文及Eurocae ED-324标准设计学习保证流程；提出一套机器学习需求（ML Requirements）和机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description）；模型采用长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络架构；最后在传统航电计算机（legacy avionics computers）上验证需求是否被满足。

Result: 所提出的机器学习需求和模型描述在LSTM模型实现上得到验证；该实现已成功在传统（旧型）航电计算机上演示运行，证明其计算资源需求满足机载约束，能够支持关键功能的部署，如机载起飞重量告警。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一个符合航空适航学习保证框架（EASA概念文件与Eurocae ED-324）的端到端ML开发与验证流程，验证了LSTM重量估计器在传统机载硬件上的工程可行性，为机器学习模型在安全关键机载系统中的部署提供了一条可审定、可复用的途径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [94] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的三角模糊数距离度量——三角模糊重标定距离（d_TR），它将线性重标定直接嵌入距离计算中，从而在比较模糊数时自动完成归一化，无需外部预处理。作者证明了该距离满足度量公理（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并具备有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，适用于异构模糊数据应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策常需处理不精确或不确定信息，通常用模糊集特别是三角模糊数（TFN）来表示。许多模糊方法的核心是量化TFN之间的距离。然而，现有距离度量大多假设所有数值处于同一尺度，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时，需要预先进行归一化处理，这增加了流程复杂性和误差来源。因此需要一种将归一化内置于距离计算的度量。

Method: 提出d_TR度量，其关键在于将线性重标定（LRE）直接集成到距离计算之中，在模糊数比较过程中即完成归一化。作者从数学上证明d_TR满足度量的全部性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并进一步证明其有界性、尺度不变性与原点不变性。此外，通过引入权重向量可实现维度优先级的排序。

Result: 形式化地证明了d_TR是一个真正的度量（metric），满足非负性、同一性、对称性及三角不等式；同时证明了其有界性、尺度不变性（scale-invariant）和原点不变性（origin-invariant），并可结合权重向量对维度进行加权。

Conclusion: 结合上述性质与维度加权机制，d_TR适用于涉及异构模糊数据的各类应用场景，例如合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助，解决了传统距离度量在异构属性下需要外部归一化预处理的局限性。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [95] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K，一个大规模多模态动态心电数据集（788份临床Holter记录、22,980个问答对，具有信号-视频-文本三模态对齐），并基于此构建了Holtercare-Bench多模态基准，用于评估模型在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结方面的能力。评测发现主流MLLM在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距，而微调能带来大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型在医学应用中表现出色，但多数模型偏向静态图像或短期信号。在动态心电（ECG）这一关键领域，模型难以进行复杂的时间推理和诊断报告生成，主要原因是缺乏高质量的数据集和基准。现有工作未覆盖动态心电的长期时序特性，亟需构建专门的资源来推动该方向研究。

Method: 作者构建了两项核心资源：(1) Holtercare-23K数据集，从788份临床Holter记录中生成22,980个问答对，创新性地实现信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；(2) Holtercare-Bench基准，从时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度系统评估多模态模型在动态心电上的能力。评测采用零样本测试与微调两种方式对比代表性MLLM的表现。

Result: 零样本评测表明，主流MLLM在处理超长病理序列时存在显著的性能差距，难以胜任动态心电的时间推理与诊断报告任务；而在Holtercare-23K上微调后，代表性模型的性能获得大幅提升，验证了数据集的有效性和基准的区分度。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLM在电生理学领域的局限性，为长期医学多模态大模型提供了基础性基准资源，填补了动态心电方向高质量数据集与评测标准的空白，并公开了项目代码。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [96] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究評估量子-古典混合機器學習應用於血液游離DNA（cfDNA）片段體學與甲基化資料的肺癌偵測。在特徵篩選後以20與40特徵子集訓練模型，使用角度與稠密角度特徵映射將特徵編碼到量子希爾伯特空間，搭配多種糾纏策略，以精確態向量模擬計算基於保真度的量子核，並整合預計算核SVM與核PCA邏輯迴歸，與原始特徵SVM比較。結果顯示量子核模型在片段體學上多個20特徵配置優於古典SVM基準，在甲基化上古典SVM仍最佳但部分量子模型具競爭力且特異性提升；特徵數從20增至40未一致改善且增加變異。整體支持量子核方法為cfDNA肺癌偵測之具前景途徑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低劑量胸部電腦斷層肺癌篩檢可降低死亡率，但受限於接受度、遵從度與管理挑戰；血液cfDNA生物標記為互補方案，然而肺癌異質性與高維度非線性分子訊號使早期偵測困難，故需探索能處理此類複雜資料的機器學習方法。

Method: 採用量子-古典混合機器學習：先進行特徵選擇，以20與40特徵子集建模；將特徵以角度與稠密角度特徵映射編碼至量子希爾伯特空間，並使用多種糾纏策略；以精確態向量模擬計算保真度量子核，結合預計算核SVM與核PCA邏輯迴歸；以原始特徵SVM做為基準進行比較。

Result: 在重複留出評估中，量子核模型在兩個資料集均達競爭性表現。片段體學方面，多個20特徵配置的AUC優於古典SVM基準，顯示能有效捕捉非線性cfDNA片段化結構；甲基化方面，古典SVM取得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具競爭力且在某些情況提升特異性。特徵數由20增至40並未一致改善表現，且常增加變異性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法可作為cfDNA肺癌偵測之有前景工具，尤其對片段體學非線性特徵有潛在優勢；未來需進一步最佳化編碼與糾纏設計，並探討特徵數與穩定性之間的取捨。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [97] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用目标任务数据集来构建简单分类器以提升LLM不确定性量化（UQ）性能的方法。该分类器以现有零样本不确定性打分及相似查询的正确性为特征，预测LLM回答是否正确，在不增加大量计算开销的情况下稳定超越现有的零样本UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化对LLM的安全部署至关重要。现有方法（从口头表达置信度到需要多次采样的方法）大多是零样本的，无需标注数据即可产生不确定性分数；但实际部署前仍需在目标数据集上评估其性能。作者希望利用这一被忽视的评估数据集来进一步提升UQ效果。

Method: 利用目标数据集构建简单的分类器，以现有零样本不确定性分数以及相似查询的正确性作为特征，预测LLM回答的正确性。该方法在现有UQ分数基础上叠加，计算开销极小。

Result: 实验表明，借助数据集构建的分类器能够一致地优于现有的零样本不确定性分数，同时保持极低的计算开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法是一种廉价且简单的增强方案，可作为现实应用中LLM不确定性量化的实用改进手段，兼顾性能与部署效率。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [98] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机理断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将机制可解释性中的多种测量手段（激活修补、梯度、Hessian-向量积、子集干预）统一表述为 y = Ax + w 的线性测量结构，并在控制任务（两HMM模型）与真实模型（Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI、Qwen-2.5-7B）上给出"由低代价测量起步、在留出干预上校验、按残余误差扩充测量族"的实践流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性的各类方法（patching、gradients、Hessian-vector products、subset interventions）在访问假设、目标量上各不相同，缺少统一的测量结构与误差语言，导致无法系统比较其代价、覆盖范围与可信度，也难以判断何时需要更昂贵的测量族。

Method: 在选定基与干预族后，将测量统一写成 y = Ax + w 的形式（A为干预描述，x为目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差与基误设）。由此推导实践流程：先用代价最低的测量，在目标尺度上的留出干预上检验，校准简单偏差，当存在结构化残差时再扩充测量族（如提升测量、Hessian-向量积）。同时以控制任务作为观测器式验证场景。

Result: 两HMM模型中，控制误差随观测误差上升，目标改善可能掩盖隐状态移动；前向访问下稀疏聚合测量比坐标修补用更少干预恢复有限效应映射，梯度访问下有限探针改善局部归因映射；提升测量与Hessian-向量积可恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互，且Tracr表明所需测量族依赖基的选择。GPT-2-small IOI 上 Name Mover–Negative Name Mover 交互是三个跨组对中最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B 上经有限校准的加性拒答-响应映射已足够，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 统一的测量结构能指导测量代价与验证的权衡，但"需要何种测量族"随模型、基和任务而异，应先验证再决定是否提升测量复杂度，而非默认需要高阶交互项。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [99] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板加速的不完备神经网络鲁棒性验证的有效性及其局限，提出联合稳定神经元指标解释模板包含率的差异性，并设计了FastCert技术自动跨层分发模板或完全弃用模板，在L0验证任务上平均加速1.13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在关键领域广泛应用，鲁棒性验证日益重要。模板复用/证明共享可在某些场景加速不完备验证，但其效果在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下是否稳健仍不清楚，需要系统研究其在何种条件下有效、以及何时几乎不可能加速。

Method: 首先进行系统性实证研究，分析模板子sumption率（包含率）在不同场景下的变化；提出“联合稳定神经元”（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板加速效果的差异。基于该分析，提出FastCert：自动将模板分发到神经网络不同层以提升性能影响，并在模板不太可能产生加速时完全跳过模板复用。

Result: 研究表明模板包含率在不同场景下差异很大，联合稳定神经元指标能解释为何某些情况下模板技术几乎无法提供加速。在大规模基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上，FastCert较现有模板复用技术实现平均1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速并非普遍有效，其效果受限于网络与数据特性；通过量化联合稳定神经元可以预判模板复用收益，而FastCert通过自动化的模板分发与弃用策略，能够稳健地提升不完整验证的加速效果。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [100] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)框架，一种纵向贝叶斯学习方法，用于从纵向扩散张量成像（DTI）数据中连续估计阿尔茨海默病（AD）的疾病严重程度，推导出疾病连续体评分（DCS）以量化个体在AD连续体上的位置及其不确定性，并在ADNI队列上验证其优于现有疾病进展方法且具有临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD本质上是连续的生物学过程，但现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续进展，限制了纵向动态建模能力。

Method: DCP将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，联合整合纵向观测与弱临床监督（Bayesian Learning框架），并从中推导Disease Continuum Score (DCS)来量化个体在AD连续体中的位置及其不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明DCP持续优于代表性疾病进展方法。综合分析验证表明DCS能准确刻画疾病严重程度、展现强临床相关性、保持纵向疾病演化规律，并能预测未来疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种基于影像的定量表示，可超越传统诊断标签和临床评分，实现AD进展的连续评估，有望作为疾病连续状态的成像衍生生物标志物。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [101] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EventTime，一个多分辨率事件条件影响预测框架，结合长时市场背景、短时事件前动态与事件元数据，并配合动态对比学习目标，用于预测网络安全漏洞披露后的短期异常损失；同时构建了SECURE数据集验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全漏洞披露等冲击会突然扰乱金融时间序列并造成显著异常损失，但多数时序预测模型只关注趋势、季节性、自相关等内生规律，难以处理稀有且异质的外部事件；加之高影响事件稀疏及背景市场噪声，事件条件下的短期负面影响预测极具挑战。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，融合长期市场上下文、短期事件前动态与事件元数据；设计事件融合模块将时序表示与事件属性耦合并识别相关的近期市场模式；为缓解稀疏监督，引入动态对比学习目标，训练中构造事件与时间序列感知的正负样本对；并构建SECURE数据集，将网络安全事件与股票时序及结构化/LLM语义特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估计事件后财务损失上持续优于最先进的时序预测与事件感知基线；进一步分析显示其表示更具事件敏感性、对不完整元数据更鲁棒，且对网络安全披露后的短期市场影响估计更可解释。

Conclusion: EventTime有效解决了事件条件下短期异常损失预测问题，证明显式耦合事件元数据与多分辨率时序上下文、结合动态对比学习能显著提升稀疏高影响事件上的预测性能，为安全事件驱动的金融影响分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [102] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，将表格数据的正常状态知识（统计摘要、因果依赖、蒸馏原型）组织成prompt生成评分引擎代码，实现对异常样本的检测，无需微调和神经网络训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本通常表现为跨特征依赖关系的违背，而非简单的边缘分布偏离。现有检测器依赖几何或重构信号，而基于LLM的方法主要依赖正常样本微调或生成合成异常，未能充分挖掘LLM对结构化知识的推理能力。

Method: LLM-Detector框架：将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，组织成结构化prompt用于代码生成；生成的评分引擎综合统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常三个信号，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上对比15个SOTA基线，在混合类型和连续型数据上均取得一致的性能提升；同时无需LLM微调或神经网络训练，显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector验证了以prompt条件化的结构化评分合成为核心的设计思路，使LLM能够从正常状态知识中推导异常检测逻辑，提供了高效且实用的现实世界表格数据异常检测方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [103] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的系统模型（显式考虑重训预算与训练+部署延迟）对周期性重训、误差阈值触发、ADWIN统计漂移触发三种策略与不重训基线进行了大规模对照实验（3,933次运行）。核心发现是：最关键的设计决策不是重训策略，而是部署模型是否支持增量学习。在线增量学习下，54组成对比较中没有任何策略与基线存在实际显著差异；而在无增量学习时，策略选择使漂移后准确率相差15-55个百分点，其中周期性重训在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两种反应式策略，反应式策略仅在循环漂移下保持优势。研究还记录了反应式策略的系统性失效模式，以及一种会静默减半有效重训预算的延迟-预算排队交互，并公开了完整模拟器、数据管道与逐次运行产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但从业者缺乏关于何时重训的原则性指导。重训成本高昂、预算有限，且重训后的模型无法立即生效——训练与部署延迟会让陈旧模型在数据持续变化期间继续提供预测，因此需要明确的实证依据来指导重训决策。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一的系统模型，将重训预算和训练+部署延迟显式化，对三种实用模型刷新策略（周期性重训、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发重训）与不重训基线进行受控实证研究。实验覆盖3,933次运行，包含三种漂移机制、三种预算水平、最多五种延迟水平、三个数据集和两种学习模式。

Result: 研究发现：单一最具影响力的设计决策并非重训策略，而是部署模型是否进行增量学习。对于带即时标签的线性在线学习器，采用逐样本增量更新时，54组成对比较中没有任何策略与不重训基线存在实际显著差异，即使延迟极端时也是如此。若无增量更新，策略选择会使漂移后准确率产生15-55个百分点的差距：简单的周期性重训在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两种反应式策略，而反应式策略仅在循环漂移下保有优势。此外还发现了反应式策略的系统性失效模式，以及一种延迟-预算排队交互会静默地将有效重训预算减半。

Conclusion: 作者得出实践指导：重训策略的选择应首先取决于模型是否具备增量学习能力；在不具备增量学习时，周期性重训是更稳健的默认选择，反应式策略需谨慎部署。为支持可复现性，作者开源了完整模拟器、数据集处理管道及逐次运行的工件。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [104] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DeltaMomentum：一种将方向感知能力直接构建进动量更新规则（而非仅在缓冲区周围包裹额外处理）的优化器组件。它利用线性层梯度天然具有的"输入作key、输出侧误差作value"的键值结构，用规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲区，使每个方向以其出现频率决定的速率被遗忘。该模块可即插即用地替换任意优化器的动量缓冲，且DeltaAdamW在FineWeb-Edu预训练中相较AdamW显著节省步数（67M下最多46.39%，370M下22.12%），增益在1B与Chinchilla最优预算下仍持续，对SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny在CIFAR-10上同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器将动量实现为过去梯度的指数移动平均（EMA），以固定速率遗忘所有方向。但深度网络训练中输入的分布高度各向异性：少数方向被频繁查询，多数方向极少出现。现有方法只是在该缓冲区外包裹额外处理，动量更新本身未变。作者因此希望让动量更新规则本身具备"方向感知"能力，按各方向的出现频率自适应地决定遗忘速率。

Method: 核心观察：线性层的梯度可分解为输入（作为key）和输出侧误差（作为value）。基于这一键值结构，DeltaMomentum利用规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲区：每个方向都以"它自身出现的频率"所决定的速率被遗忘。作者证明其是合法动量（与标准EMA定义相容）、无需矩阵求逆即可施加输入侧曲率校正，并在固定/漂移最优解下都比EMA更快清除陈旧方向。作为即插即用组件，其系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移，额外计算量仅为gated-MLP线性成本的22.2%–25.0%，且无需持久化内存。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，使用DeltaMomentum的AdamW（DeltaAdamW）在三个种子下达到AdamW验证损失时最多节省46.39±4.32%步数（67M规模）和22.12±0.80%步数（370M规模），增益在1B的Chinchilla最优预算下依然保持。同期调的Muon基线在两种语言模型规模上均高于DeltaAdamW；SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny在CIFAR-10上的增益同样成立。训练时诊断证实了预测机制（更好的梯度追踪与更健康的输入方向）。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum表明：动量更新本身可以从数据的各向异性结构中获益，将方向感知内建进更新规则是一种有效、可迁移且计算开销可控的优化改进，可作为任意优化器的动量缓冲的直接替代，并在多种模型规模与任务上带来一致且显著的加速收敛。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget.
